## Miss explainer

| reason | n | what would fix it |
|---|---|---|
| not-on-tracked-token | 339 | track the unit on nameplate/softenemy tokens through the Identity ledger (token->GUID) and feed it to the HSM |
| no-api-signal | 166 | engine limit: no live event carries this (use C_DamageMeter totals after combat, or accept the gap) |
| target-changed | 118 | keep the previous target GUID for 0.4 s after PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED (ledger 'token' entry) and match against both |
| secret-payload | 82 | signal arrives but its payload is secret: resolve through the Ledger (cached clear value) or elimination |
| wrong-attribution | 29 | attributed to the wrong source: tighten the rule (cast-frame / DM per-source) for this category |
| timing-window | 11 | the live signal exists but outside the matching window: widen the rule window (see the observed lag) |
| concurrent-source | 11 | another source hit the same unit in the same window: use cast-frame evidence or the DM per-source delta |
| recorder-throttle | 9 | the HSM had it, the offline recorder dropped it (5/s cap): raise RecordDB cap for this event or rely on CombatLogDB |
| amount-mismatch | 2 | a live signal is in the window with a different amount (partial absorb/resist/overkill): match with tolerance |

## Co-occurrence miner (tools/fdump/correlate.py)

Captures: run10. 1-hop windows 50/150/300/750 ms; higher-order adds 1.5 s and depth <= 4 (profile full). Lift uses add-½ smoothing (finite, with CI); status = cross-run / held-1 / provisional / failed. Tokens are canonicalized (alias collapse) before mining.


## Distinct findings (v2.2.5)

One row per real finding after token-alias collapse, lift smoothing, chain minimality, and echo filtering. Status: **cross-run** = held on ≥2 other captures; **held-1** = held on 1 other; **provisional** = single-run / one fight; **failed** = holdout failed. Proposed live rules are review-only unless marked already live.

### Reasoning and proposed rules

**1. [run10 / elimination]** a live hit on my target has no matching own-cast frame and no DamageMeter update in that frame, so after subtracting what I can claim the remainder is someone else (ELIM(no my-cast, no DM):UNIT_COMBAT / target / arg1=WOUND -> SPELL_DAMAGE / player>player)

> proposed: Elimination: UNIT_COMBAT damage on target with no my-cast and no DAMAGE_METER update in-frame -> src=other; reconcile post-combat as live total - C_DamageMeter mine.

## v2.2.4 semantic lint + reality cross-check (standing check list)

Every build: stored descriptions re-parsed with the current parser; rules: lockout/interrupt text never becomes auraDuration; summon lifetimes/pulses and imbue durations never become aura or cast amounts; every 'N sec/min' is classified (aura / lockout / stun / summon / imbue / period / header) or counted ambiguous; multi-cost spells keep every cost; tooltip header fields agree with the structured APIs; a parsed aura duration never observed is 'unverified'.
